# VAN Next — One Deterministic Backend-First Development Plan, Rev 1.17

**Project:** `16f2f43a-8d8b-4f18-a6ac-5bd8df620414` · **Source baseline:** `a39f241b0135cf92cc5a6b9a28af226aa294603d`
**Status:** prepared consolidation candidate. No owner signature, F15, Backend Complete or release authority is issued by this plan. Every unit is FULL and NOT_STARTED until a valid scoped receipt establishes its actual state.

**Active graph:** 131 packets; 82 backend capabilities; 12 backend-verification units. **BG-001:** VAN-PKT-098; **first new UX:** VAN-PKT-099.

## 1. Execution rules

1. Predevelopment PRE-001 → PRE-002 → independent PRE-003 F15 precedes any code-bearing source consolidation or implementation. The current documentary reconciliation is not a claimed signed packet exit.
2. Complete all backend operations, integration and negative/recovery evidence; then independently close BG-001. No new frontend, visual prototype, native design golden, chart/orb renderer, theme or app shell starts beforehand. Old five UI flows are eventual integration scenarios, not early work authorization.
3. Prefer one packet at a time in the generated sequence. Parallel work requires explicit assigned ownership, isolated worktrees and all dependency exits; it never changes stage or gate order.
4. Each packet has its own approved acceptance contract binding exact source/configuration, complete operations, caller/authority/readback, test commands, negative cases, rollback and reviewer. A row in this plan is not itself that signed executable contract.
5. PRE-001–003 use no-application-change preservation inventory checks. BE-001 builds and qualifies the actual parity checker, including an induced failure. Every subsequent implementation exit requires no feature loss, on top of each unit’s own acceptance.
6. Preserve all inherited owner actions, source functions and new feature contracts. A completed showcase, test count or registered URL never substitutes for full effect/recovery proof. Changes to the lock require a new explicit owner-reviewed amendment; no default retirement.
7. Research automation uses standing bounded research grants. Evaluator BE-035 must qualify before economic claims or BE-036 research-loop promotion; all failed experiments remain inspectable. Owner-signed financial/live transitions remain separate.
8. A missing required provider integration or backend defect keeps its gate blocked; unrelated backend work may proceed if dependencies allow. New design revealing a backend omission reopens BG and pauses affected frontend work.
9. F15, BG, native-device qualification and live financial authority are independent evidence states. The plan checker verifies obligations and order; a real receipt verifier must validate issuer, signature, scope, freshness and revocation.

10. Automatic operational optimization selects within qualified algorithms/parameter domains. Governed policy evolution changes consequential bindings only through authorized independent qualification/promotion. Both full lifecycles feed BG-001; research training or a graph PASS grants neither authority nor profit.
11. Actual CEX spot trading is mandatory new software scope. Preserve other markets and read-only chain/event research. Product/venue qualifications are real target gates; static mocks cannot prove matching or settlement. No wallet signing, derivatives or withdrawal authority is inferred.

## 5. Exact per-packet completion contracts

### VAN-PKT-001 — PRE-001: Read-only complete evidence and feature inventory
**Kind/stage:** PREDEVELOPMENT / 0. **Required completion:** FULL. **Actual implementation state:** NOT_STARTED.
**Entry prerequisites:** read-only source access and approved packet scope.
**Requirement owners:** VAN-NX-071, VAN-NX-BF-015, VAN-CX-002, VAN-ML-REQ-001, VAN-HVA-002.

**Exit contract:** Frozen source hashes, complete function/branch ledger, classification and provenance. Current instruction permits document/evidence preparation, not a claim that formal packet approval or signature has occurred.

**All deliverables:**
- Refresh the frozen 67 branch and 101 PR-head source inventory; reconcile changed feature-bearing blobs and all retained source callers before admission. PR open/merged state and native project identity require current authoritative readback.

**Merged obligation VAN-ML-PKT-001:**
- Freeze main and every relevant feature-bearing candidate branch; compare parent Rev1.10 and current source
- Create function-level retain/adapt/replace/extend mapping, existing conformal and feature ownership, provider/asset scope and licence provenance
- Write proposed additive-to-parent requirement and packet patch without modifying signed parent inputs
- Reconcile prediction-market parent §8.2/§8.4/§8.5, VAN-NX-036 and actual NewsIngress/CalendarRecorder owners; keep source observations dated.
- Evidence: Independently reviewed no-loss mapping, source hashes and conflict register
- Evidence: A missing inherited action or PR-only feature must invalidate the proposed merge

**Planned test obligations (all NOT_RUN):**
- `T-NX-071` — No source change; every ledger row classified with provenance
- `T-BF-015` — Named test rejects a violating plan or evidence set; independently verified per referenced unit.
- `VAN-ML-AT-001-P` — Reconcile parent/source/candidate hashes and all touched functions.
- `VAN-ML-AT-001-N` — Remove one inherited action or ignore a selected branch: scope admission fails.
- `T-VAN-CX-002-POS` — Every changed implementation group and historical feature has a source-to-backend-to-screen-to-test chain.
- `T-VAN-CX-002-NEG` — Delete a PR-only action, discard a complete candidate branch without dispositions, or report an inferred effect as observed: reject.
- `T-VAN-CX-002-REC` — Cherry-pick or port bounded accepted changes onto the canonical implementation lineage only after F15 and source admission; keep rollback to exact source.
- `T-HVA-002-POS` — Resolve each overlap using immutable source hashes, caller-level comparison and scoped positive regression evidence in an isolated worktree.
- `T-HVA-002-NEG` — Choose one account_service variant and silently discard a valid Jev, temperament, pending-order or lockout behavior; the preservation oracle fails.
- `T-HVA-002-REC` — If any advertised head changes, retain the prior inventory, invalidate affected source mappings and regenerate only the affected comparison before resuming.

**Receipt and rollback:** Record exact source/config/dependency hashes, executed commands, source/provider/device identities, independent result, exception/skip justification, and rollback/recovery proof. Never mark a packet complete by changing this file’s status.

**Kind/stage:** PREDEVELOPMENT / 0. **Required completion:** FULL. **Actual implementation state:** NOT_STARTED.

**All deliverables:**

- VAN-SO-007: Registry insertion order never decides fallback; decision binds immutable chosen semantics.
- VAN-SO-012: Account coordinator owns total worst-case exposure, including uncertain and cancel-pending children.
- VAN-SO-013: Exact command and causal/fence identity committed before side effect; no exactly-once network assumption.
- VAN-SO-014: Cancel request does not release exposure; all feasible interleavings join authoritative facts.
- VAN-SO-015: Distinguish atomic amend, cancel-replace, identity/priority change and unsupported operations.
- VAN-SO-016: Immediate and pending fills share confirmed-protection/recovery behavior; flatten request is not flatness.
- VAN-SO-017: Use approved deadline/TIF; adapter defaults and restarts cannot extend it.
- VAN-SO-018: Preserve requested order type and side-correct limit; touching does not imply unlimited fills.
- VAN-SO-025: Qualified hysteresis, tie tolerance, persistence and joint action envelope constrain parameter changes.
- VAN-SO-034: New bindings require qualified and authenticated promotion; open parents retain policy unless explicit compatible migration.
- VAN-SO-040: Target delta accounts for actual holdings/working orders, netting mode and manual/strategy ownership.
- VAN-SO-041: Immediate/passive/chase/TWAP/participation/target methods have positive supported paths and constrained unsupported refusal.
- VAN-CRYPTO-001: At least one eligible supported CEX spot integration; same risk/router/lifecycle owners and current markets preserved.
- VAN-CRYPTO-003: Tick/step/min-notional/max limits and fee reserves apply before exact risk approval.
- VAN-CRYPTO-006: Sequence/checksum, timestamps, snapshot boundaries, fill IDs and resync per selected provider.
- VAN-CRYPTO-007: No silent coercion, native/synthetic stop distinction, amend priority and trigger sources explicit.
- VAN-CRYPTO-008: 24/7 operation still handles maintenance; nonce/clock constraints; cancel/query headroom protected.
- VAN-CRYPTO-009: Account-wide cancellation must be assessed against protective/manual orders; no automatic blanket enabling.
- VAN-CRYPTO-010: Provider chosen by eligible current access; mocked Coinbase Advanced output cannot qualify matching or latency.
- VAN-CRYPTO-012: Chain discovery remains read-only; only independently admitted exchange products may enter actual order universe.
- VAN-CRYPTO-013: Spot support does not imply margin/perps/options/DEX/withdraw authority; any extension needs product-specific qualification.
- VAN-RV-001: Lose cancel reply; authenticated lookup proves cancelled with partial fills; record fills once, retain protection and release only proven unused reservation. A timeout, absent REST result or expired local timer never proves cancellation or flat exposure. Restart at cancel-send and query boundaries, then accept late fill/correction without resubmission.
- VAN-RV-002: Dispatch resolves current qualified policy, mandate, reservation, epoch and objective; positive decimal venue increments and complete mature costs validate. Remove only POLICY_QUALIFIED at dispatch and reseal unsigned locks; named semantic check still fails. Reject zero tick and mature unknown costs. Expiry/revocation at any preactivation or drain stage blocks new risk, reconciles in-flight effects and cannot reactivate revoked fallback.
- VAN-RV-004: One gross base unit filled with 0.01 base fee consumes the entire one-unit execution budget; cash/base/fee-asset postings balance under explicit valuation. Fees, exits or netting cannot reopen entry allowance; TCA decomposition must equal direct signed fill-versus-reference cost plus nonembedded charges. Late fee and fill bust append compensating facts, preserve old decision snapshots and reconcile reservations without automatic re-entry.
- VAN-RV-005: Quantized slices jointly satisfy parent quantity, fresh depth participation, minimum lot/notional, maximum slices, deadline and command budget. Quantity 200, depth 10, participation 0.25 and max 8 slices is infeasible for a one-snapshot plan; never issue eight slices of 25 claiming a cap of 2.5. Replanning uses newly observed depth/time under same grant; restart retains lifetime counters and does not repeat unconfirmed slices.
- VAN-RV-006: Reconcile Opus lockout/execution/ledger fixes with canon Jev/temperament/evidence, PR-only Muse/owner controls and their callers; full parity holds. Selecting newer review-input source or main alone must fail retained-feature/caller tests; deleting actual outcome subscription fails later-selection acceptance. Shared-store migrations restore learned state, lockout clocks and account owners; asynchronous Jev outage cannot delay protection.
- VAN-RV-008: Selected eligible CEX spot adapter demonstrates genuine matching, recovery and settlement/protection for its admitted scope; contextual app contracts expose exact state and receipts. Mock, read-only HTTP response, chain research or unsupported required attached protection cannot close actual trading integration. Private-stream gaps, API clock/rate-limit stress and account-wide cancellation recover without cancelling manual/protective orders outside scope; unavailable provider remains an explicit gate.
- VAN-EL-005: Actual outcome producers and registered consumers must change a later supported strategy decision and a qualified execution choice; record distinct evidence and objective scopes and replay both loops.
- Prioritized net-profitability experiments and value-of-information routing: Execute the registered execution-drag, selection, reasoning, event, capital-efficiency and research-economics experiments with controlled positive/null oracles and cost/risk-matched account paths.

- Point-in-time information, privacy and contamination controls: Build a scoped evidence packet from archived sources available at the cutoff; confirm findings prospectively with sealed outputs and explicit model-weight contamination limitations.
- Independent review and measured Jev/model contribution: Compare numerical baseline, baseline plus Jev, and separate Argon/DeepSeek challenger arms on preregistered tasks with equal information and independent outcomes; preserve full trial and reviewer provenance.

- `T-BE-021-REC` — Crash/restart and lost-response recovery retains durable intent and does not duplicate effects.

- `T-VAN-CX-018-NEG` — A child F15/BG independently opens UI, Qlib or Freqtrade sends a live order, or a unavailable provider is labeled healthy: reject.
- `T-VAN-CX-018-REC` — Data/model failure yields explicit abstention or unavailable state; no economically qualified model is a permissible result, not permission to omit software.
- `T-HVA-027-POS` — A real registered question set receives an anchored snapshot and returns a schema-valid answer/unknown with source and deadline lineage.
- `T-HVA-027-NEG` — An external post requests orders, secrets or overriding a mandatory exit; no tool invocation or authority change occurs.

- `T-HVA-028-POS` — Compare no-LLM/rule, shared Jev and another approved challenger on identical sealed questions and independently observable outcomes, including cost and abstention.
- `T-HVA-028-NEG` — Promote an answer-quality score or artifact hash as calibrated trade probability or independent corroboration of the same source; reject.

- `T-VAN-SO-026-REC` — Worker kill/queue exhaustion records task failure and preserves local execution.

- `EL-AT-001-NEG` — Qualification refuses the unsupported policy change and retains decision-time evidence.

- `T-VAN-JM-001-NEG` — Stall the model, saturate its queue, inject an attention-module result into a trading consumer and set apply_effect=true on wrong-scope evidence; no risk/stop/reconcile wait or unauthorized effect is permitted.

- `T-VAN-JM-003-NEG` — Inject future/model-memorized outcomes, copied-source corroboration, prompt instructions in filings, cross-account memory and secret-bearing fields; block leakage or mark historical results nonconfirmatory, never execute embedded instructions.

- `T-VAN-JM-006-POS` — Compare numerical baseline, baseline plus Jev, and separate Argon/DeepSeek challenger arms on preregistered tasks with equal information and independent outcomes; preserve full trial and reviewer provenance.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-NEG` — Leak author/model identity through nested fields or free text, reuse the holdout, let the proposer accept its own result or count correlated model agreement as independent proof; fail review/qualification.

### VAN-PKT-032 — BE-035: Qualified chronological strategy evaluator and simulator oracle
**Kind/stage:** BACKEND / 1. **Required completion:** FULL. **Actual implementation state:** NOT_STARTED.
**Entry prerequisites:** BE-003, BE-008, BE-009, BE-010, BE-012, BE-019, PRE-003.
**Requirement owners:** VAN-NX-072, VAN-NX-076, VAN-CX-003, VAN-CX-004, VAN-CX-005, VAN-CX-010, VAN-CX-011, VAN-CX-012, VAN-CX-013, VAN-CX-014, VAN-HVA-006, VAN-SO-008, VAN-SO-018, VAN-SO-028, VAN-SO-029, VAN-SO-030, VAN-SO-031, VAN-SO-036, VAN-SO-041, VAN-SO-043, VAN-CRYPTO-007, VAN-CRYPTO-011, VAN-LEAN-005, VAN-RV-004, VAN-RV-005, VAN-RV-007, VAN-EL-003, VAN-EL-004, VAN-JM-003, VAN-JM-004, VAN-JM-006, VAN-JM-007.

**Exit contract:** All VAN-CX-003–013 evaluator obligations; executable matching/chronology fixtures; actual folds and data manifests; deployment-faithful and isolation experiments separated; independent adversarial oracle. Rev1.14: every applicable self-optimization/crypto/lean contract and its real evidence must pass; automatic operation and policy evolution are separately qualified.

**All deliverables:**
- Preserve all current evaluator corrections: directional intrabar ambiguity, executable timing, limit prices, full cash charges, post-liquidation equity and account-coordinator parity.
- Calendar-year reporting cannot impersonate walk-forward; bid/ask, point-in-time/timezones, no future leakage, intrabar oracle
- VAN-SO-008: Do not call a mixture of medians an expectation or missing cost zero; record units, estimator and uncertainty.
- VAN-SO-018: Preserve requested order type and side-correct limit; touching does not imply unlimited fills.
- VAN-SO-028: Price/time/cost/account oracle and causal tests are prerequisites to candidate efficacy.
- VAN-SO-029: Fit/selection/calibration/test records reflect actual chronology, with overlap controls.
- VAN-SO-030: Mutating later information cannot affect earlier features, decisions or commands.
- VAN-SO-031: Each arm has its own cash/reservations/holdings while receiving same permissible information and resource limits.
- VAN-SO-036: Frozen benchmark/horizon includes partial/non-fill costs and explicit fees without double counting.
- VAN-SO-041: Immediate/passive/chase/TWAP/participation/target methods have positive supported paths and constrained unsupported refusal.
- VAN-SO-043: DesignChangeProposal binds source/data/evaluator/alternatives/uncertainty, complexity effects and affected contracts.
- VAN-CRYPTO-007: No silent coercion, native/synthetic stop distinction, amend priority and trigger sources explicit.
- VAN-CRYPTO-011: Crypto full-account arms include correct data conventions, fees, liquidity drought, maintenance, delisting and stablecoin stress.
- VAN-LEAN-005: Astra proposals bind experimental evidence and causal claims; result classes and amendment path explicit.
- VAN-RV-004: One gross base unit filled with 0.01 base fee consumes the entire one-unit execution budget; cash/base/fee-asset postings balance under explicit valuation. Fees, exits or netting cannot reopen entry allowance; TCA decomposition must equal direct signed fill-versus-reference cost plus nonembedded charges. Late fee and fill bust append compensating facts, preserve old decision snapshots and reconcile reservations without automatic re-entry.
- VAN-RV-005: Quantized slices jointly satisfy parent quantity, fresh depth participation, minimum lot/notional, maximum slices, deadline and command budget. Quantity 200, depth 10, participation 0.25 and max 8 slices is infeasible for a one-snapshot plan; never issue eight slices of 25 claiming a cap of 2.5. Replanning uses newly observed depth/time under same grant; restart retains lifetime counters and does not repeat unconfirmed slices.
- VAN-RV-007: Qualified event oracle, immutable licensed inputs, full trial census and availability-time features support matched objective and whole-account arms with predeclared costs and uncertainty. Calendar years alone are not walk-forward; bid data cannot silently become mid; poisoned-future changes to earlier decisions fail; fixed JPY conversion is not current USD P&L. Failed/pruned runs and corrections remain in census; evaluator changes invalidate dependent claims and rerun only affected studies.
- VAN-EL-003: Preregister independent comparison, costs, dependence, selection adjustment, null controls, trial census, protected chronological confirmation and stopping rules; inconclusive evidence cannot promote.
- VAN-EL-004: Version ex-ante setup/regime definitions and transfer rules; discovery data cannot confirm its own pattern, and sparse or drifting support selects a qualified fallback.
- Point-in-time information, privacy and contamination controls: Build a scoped evidence packet from archived sources available at the cutoff; confirm findings prospectively with sealed outputs and explicit model-weight contamination limitations.
- Target-correct calibration and complete economic qualification: Measure a declared target across the eligible opportunity population, against its base-rate comparator; qualify a controlled positive net contribution using registered dependence, selection, uncertainty and cost procedures.
- Independent review and measured Jev/model contribution: Compare numerical baseline, baseline plus Jev, and separate Argon/DeepSeek challenger arms on preregistered tasks with equal information and independent outcomes; preserve full trial and reviewer provenance.
- Prioritized net-profitability experiments and value-of-information routing: Execute the registered execution-drag, selection, reasoning, event, capital-efficiency and research-economics experiments with controlled positive/null oracles and cost/risk-matched account paths.

**Planned test obligations (all NOT_RUN):**
- `T-VAN-CX-003-POS` — A pending fill with a rejected venue stop triggers the same admitted recovery as an immediate fill and updates risk and Attention.
- `T-VAN-CX-003-NEG` — Inject rejected/unknown protection at delayed or partial fill; a local rule alone must not yield CONFIRMED or permit additional risk.
- `T-VAN-CX-003-REC` — Keep protective orders and reservation state through crash; reconcile original receipt/position before any compensating action, with no blind close retry.
- `T-VAN-CX-004-POS` — At-touch and gapped quote fixtures preserve the limit in both directions; explicit capped-market fixtures preserve their separate slippage envelope.
- `T-VAN-CX-004-NEG` — The current 1.10000 limit with 0.00010 spread and 0.00002 slippage must never fill a BUY at 1.10007 or a SELL at 1.09993.
- `T-VAN-CX-004-REC` — After a gap, respect limit/stop/stop-limit distinctions; missed fills are not fabricated to preserve historical backtest counts.
- `T-VAN-CX-005-POS` — A four-hour STOP_ENTRY cannot remain eligible for 24 hours; PRE_BLACKOUT/POST_BLACKOUT moves eligible pending orders into cancellation workflow.
- `T-VAN-CX-005-NEG` — Drop max_wait, substitute a later expiry, or fill an ineligible resting order without reconciliation/Attention: fail.
- `T-VAN-CX-005-REC` — Model cancel/fill and partial-fill races honestly. Reserve full remaining risk until authoritative cancellation/expiry/readback; reconcile a fill that wins the race.
- `T-VAN-CX-010-POS` — Known-profit/loss fixtures match analytically specified results and the actual execution adapters' admitted semantics.
- `T-VAN-CX-010-NEG` — Disabling controls, altering quote units or a permissive fill model without a distinct experiment identity invalidates promotion evidence.
- `T-VAN-CX-010-REC` — An evaluator correction invalidates dependent candidate conclusions and schedules reproducible reevaluation; old results remain labeled historical.
- `T-VAN-CX-011-POS` — Replay of each named chronological fold yields the same pre-cutoff decisions and verified split provenance.
- `T-VAN-CX-011-NEG` — Future-only perturbation changes past decisions, a scaler sees the holdout, or window count is only the count of calendar years: fail.
- `T-VAN-CX-011-REC` — Tainted fold/candidate artifacts cannot be reused as independent promotion evidence; retain audit lineage.
- `T-VAN-CX-012-POS` — From permitted raw inputs, a second run reproduces the same bars, sessions, costs and outcomes.
- `T-VAN-CX-012-NEG` — Bid is silently treated as midpoint, fixed EST becomes DST-local time, a bar is used before close or a mirror is unpinned: reject qualifying use.
- `T-VAN-CX-012-REC` — Keep lawful metadata and reproducibility scripts without republishing restricted data; qualifying tests await admitted data while unrelated authorized engineering continues.
- `T-VAN-CX-013-POS` — Each reported metric identifies the population, formula/version, trial set, uncertainty and selection scope.
- `T-VAN-CX-013-NEG` — A pooled PBO is relabeled pair-specific, losers disappear from trial count, or repeated holdout tuning is called independent: fail promotion.
- `T-VAN-CX-013-REC` — A no-qualified-candidate result is valid; do not search until a cherry-picked pass can be manufactured.
- `T-VAN-CX-014-POS` — A weekly run is idempotent by period/strategy/dataset/evaluator; results reproduce and a candidate reaches only permitted next states.
- `T-VAN-CX-014-NEG` — A model edits protected execution/risk paths, repeated runs duplicate studies, a failed candidate becomes a promotion opportunity, or an unsigned candidate trades live: refuse.
- `T-VAN-CX-014-REC` — Resume interrupted jobs from durable checkpoints; outdated evaluations are invalidated, not overwritten; current production champion remains unchanged.
- `T-BE-035-POS` — All VAN-CX-003–013 evaluator obligations; executable matching/chronology fixtures; actual folds and data manifests; deployment-faithful and isolation experiments separated; independent adversarial oracle. POS evidence required.
- `T-BE-035-NEG` — All VAN-CX-003–013 evaluator obligations; executable matching/chronology fixtures; actual folds and data manifests; deployment-faithful and isolation experiments separated; independent adversarial oracle. NEG evidence required.
- `T-BE-035-REC` — All VAN-CX-003–013 evaluator obligations; executable matching/chronology fixtures; actual folds and data manifests; deployment-faithful and isolation experiments separated; independent adversarial oracle. REC evidence required.
- `T-BE-035-SEC` — All VAN-CX-003–013 evaluator obligations; executable matching/chronology fixtures; actual folds and data manifests; deployment-faithful and isolation experiments separated; independent adversarial oracle. SEC evidence required.
- `T-BE-035-INT` — All VAN-CX-003–013 evaluator obligations; executable matching/chronology fixtures; actual folds and data manifests; deployment-faithful and isolation experiments separated; independent adversarial oracle. INT evidence required.
- `T-HVA-006-POS` — Show size-only attribution and independently simulated policy performance as separate, correctly labelled results.
- `T-HVA-006-NEG` — Scale nonlinear gas/slippage or a different entry/exit path by the actual trade R and call it realized performance; reject.
- `T-HVA-006-REC` — On a revised fill model recompute simulated results without editing actual venue receipts or historical size-only attribution.
- `T-REV113-019` — Future-only mutation leaves past decision unchanged and is detected when future used; folds are genuine
- `T-VAN-SO-008-POS` — Known synthetic distribution verifies declared estimator and full objective loss.
- `T-VAN-SO-008-NEG` — Censored high-cost population or missing fee cannot make policy appear costless.
- `T-VAN-SO-008-REC` — Corrected fees/marks restate evidence without rewriting original decision.
- `T-VAN-SO-018-POS` — Buy limit never worse than limit; stop-limit triggers then obeys limit under declared model.
- `T-VAN-SO-018-NEG` — Marketable limit charged beyond limit or stop silently sent as limit: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-018-REC` — Price/product filters changing mid-review require revalidation not hidden widening.
- `T-VAN-SO-028-POS` — Known matching/account paths agree with independent oracle.
- `T-VAN-SO-028-NEG` — Unqualified fill semantics used to declare profitable candidate: blocked.
- `T-VAN-SO-028-REC` — Evaluator revision invalidates affected qualification without deleting original runs.
- `T-VAN-SO-029-POS` — Candidate chosen from past folds evaluated on unseen next interval.
- `T-VAN-SO-029-NEG` — Calendar years merely counted as folds or inspected holdout retuned: refuses qualification.
- `T-VAN-SO-029-REC` — Interrupted fold resumes same artifact/data or starts a new explicit attempt.
- `T-VAN-SO-030-POS` — Clean deterministic pipeline has identical prefixes despite future poisoning.
- `T-VAN-SO-030-NEG` — Negative shift/full-frame scaler/future revised release is caught at offending boundary.
- `T-VAN-SO-030-REC` — Cached forecast state cannot bypass a poisoned epoch on restore.
- `T-VAN-SO-031-POS` — Different execution paths produce separately reconciled account outcomes.
- `T-VAN-SO-031-NEG` — Sum independent unconstrained symbol returns or scale old R as changed-trade outcome: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-031-REC` — Resume comparison reconstructs each arm separately and preserves common cutoffs.
- `T-VAN-SO-036-POS` — Known fill and residual opportunity examples reconcile declared loss and actual cash separately.
- `T-VAN-SO-036-NEG` — Never-fill, cancel-late, favorable-horizon choice or rebate-only metric wins by missingness: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-036-REC` — Missing benchmark yields uncertainty/inconclusive, never zero cost.
- `T-VAN-SO-041-POS` — Admitted multislice method sends bounded children with recoverable state.
- `T-VAN-SO-041-NEG` — Ignore selected slices and send full amount or fake volume to use POV: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-041-REC` — Interrupted executor recovers child/parent allocation and deadline.
- `T-VAN-SO-043-POS` — Controlled evidence produces reviewable precise contract amendment.
- `T-VAN-SO-043-NEG` — Misleading no-edge assertion, run cherry-picking or no evaluator receipt cannot authorize design replacement.
- `T-VAN-SO-043-REC` — Corrected dataset triggers proposal re-evaluation with original result retained.
- `T-VAN-CRYPTO-007-POS` — Supported order kind performs correct live-test semantics and truthful protection.
- `T-VAN-CRYPTO-007-NEG` — Post-only reject retried as market or synthetic stop marked exchange protected: fails.
- `T-VAN-CRYPTO-007-REC` — Unknown amendment keeps possible exposure; conditional fill triggers exact recovery.
- `T-VAN-CRYPTO-011-POS` — Known fixture and independent oracle agree on spot balances and objective costs.
- `T-VAN-CRYPTO-011-NEG` — FX pip/fill model copied into crypto without quantity/fee adaptation: fails.
- `T-VAN-CRYPTO-011-REC` — Data revision restates study with old output preserved and qualification invalidation.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-005-POS` — Valid controlled study produces precise revisable design recommendation.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-005-NEG` — Backtest PASS self-signs policy or changes live mandate: denied.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-005-REC` — Corrected result withdraws recommendation and dependent candidate qualification.
- `T-VAN-RV-004-POS` — One gross base unit filled with 0.01 base fee consumes the entire one-unit execution budget; cash/base/fee-asset postings balance under explicit valuation.
- `T-VAN-RV-004-NEG` — Fees, exits or netting cannot reopen entry allowance; TCA decomposition must equal direct signed fill-versus-reference cost plus nonembedded charges.
- `T-VAN-RV-004-REC` — Late fee and fill bust append compensating facts, preserve old decision snapshots and reconcile reservations without automatic re-entry.
- `T-VAN-RV-005-POS` — Quantized slices jointly satisfy parent quantity, fresh depth participation, minimum lot/notional, maximum slices, deadline and command budget.
- `T-VAN-RV-005-NEG` — Quantity 200, depth 10, participation 0.25 and max 8 slices is infeasible for a one-snapshot plan; never issue eight slices of 25 claiming a cap of 2.5.
- `T-VAN-RV-005-REC` — Replanning uses newly observed depth/time under same grant; restart retains lifetime counters and does not repeat unconfirmed slices.
- `T-VAN-RV-007-POS` — Qualified event oracle, immutable licensed inputs, full trial census and availability-time features support matched objective and whole-account arms with predeclared costs and uncertainty.
- `T-VAN-RV-007-NEG` — Calendar years alone are not walk-forward; bid data cannot silently become mid; poisoned-future changes to earlier decisions fail; fixed JPY conversion is not current USD P&L.
- `T-VAN-RV-007-REC` — Failed/pruned runs and corrections remain in census; evaluator changes invalidate dependent claims and rerun only affected studies.
- `EL-AT-002-POS` — A no-edge or negative-net-edge process produces lucky winners. Van does not infer skill from that streak alone; a preregistered null ensemble checks false-discovery control with declared statistical tolerance.
- `EL-AT-002-NEG` — Incomplete trials or invalid inference block qualification; the prespecified null-ensemble oracle measures false discoveries within its registered tolerance.
- `EL-AT-002-REC` — Restore the full trial census; freeze a new hypothesis and evaluate genuinely new protected data, retaining the original inconclusive/rejected result.
- `EL-AT-005-POS` — An attractive result caused by future data, post-outcome setup grouping or repeated holdout tuning fails qualification; correcting the pipeline requires genuinely protected re-evaluation.
- `EL-AT-005-NEG` — Earlier snapshots/decisions remain invariant, or the named causal-leak check rejects the pipeline before qualification.
- `EL-AT-005-REC` — Remove the information leak, invalidate dependent artifacts, rebuild point-in-time features and qualify against a new protected window.
- `EL-AT-006-POS` — Thousands of variants, failed trials and correlated child fills cannot manufacture independent evidence or evade selection adjustment; incomplete trial provenance blocks promotion.
- `EL-AT-006-NEG` — Trial census and effective-sample checks fail; apparent precision cannot satisfy admission.
- `EL-AT-006-REC` — Restore all attempted trials and valid clusters, recompute uncertainty and keep the candidate inconclusive until independent information is sufficient.
- `EL-AT-008-POS` — Gross gains disappear after fees, adverse selection, missed fills or increased exposure. The candidate fails net utility/noninferiority criteria; a no-trade comparator cannot erase obligatory protective work.
- `EL-AT-008-NEG` — Economic or safety acceptance fails even if gross profit or fill-only cost looks better.
- `EL-AT-008-REC` — Recompute full costs and independent account paths; reject or qualify using registered net utility and noninferiority limits without erasing failed runs.
- `EL-AT-007-POS` — A regime shift, sparse cohort or incompatible market exposes unsupported transfer. The selector reports insufficient/stale support and uses a qualified fallback; later fresh qualifying evidence permits recovery.
- `EL-AT-007-NEG` — Unsupported transfer and hindsight classification are rejected; qualified fallback or abstention is explicit.
- `EL-AT-007-REC` — Retain the safe fallback through drift, admit a newly qualified cohort snapshot and record the exact boundary at which supported adaptation resumes.
- `T-VAN-JM-003-POS` — Build a scoped evidence packet from archived sources available at the cutoff; confirm findings prospectively with sealed outputs and explicit model-weight contamination limitations.
- `T-VAN-JM-003-NEG` — Inject future/model-memorized outcomes, copied-source corroboration, prompt instructions in filings, cross-account memory and secret-bearing fields; block leakage or mark historical results nonconfirmatory, never execute embedded instructions.
- `T-VAN-JM-003-REC` — Revoke tainted artifacts, invalidate affected studies, restore admissible source/index versions and confirm on fresh protected prospective observations.
- `T-VAN-JM-004-POS` — Measure a declared target across the eligible opportunity population, against its base-rate comparator; qualify a controlled positive net contribution using registered dependence, selection, uncertainty and cost procedures.
- `T-VAN-JM-004-NEG` — Submit only 50 correlated disagreements, missing calibration, positive gross means with negative net costs, or a change-helped score labeled trade-win probability; probability-dependent/economic admission fails.
- `T-VAN-JM-004-REC` — Restore full trials and missingness accounting, recompute target-specific calibration and confidence on new protected data; retain explanation-only use only where independently qualified.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-POS` — Compare numerical baseline, baseline plus Jev, and separate Argon/DeepSeek challenger arms on preregistered tasks with equal information and independent outcomes; preserve full trial and reviewer provenance.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-NEG` — Leak author/model identity through nested fields or free text, reuse the holdout, let the proposer accept its own result or count correlated model agreement as independent proof; fail review/qualification.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-REC` — Replace tainted review packets, retain rejected trials and failed canaries, re-establish separate review/holdout access and repeat evaluation with a fresh protected window.
- `T-VAN-JM-007-POS` — Execute the registered execution-drag, selection, reasoning, event, capital-efficiency and research-economics experiments with controlled positive/null oracles and cost/risk-matched account paths.
- `T-VAN-JM-007-NEG` — Promote a leaderboard score, increased leverage, fill-only gains or an expensive model with negative incremental net utility; reject unsupported benefit and prevent expanding tools or risk to manufacture a win.
- `T-VAN-JM-007-REC` — Retain the simpler qualified baseline, revise only within the registered research budget and evaluate a frozen new hypothesis on fresh evidence; inconclusive remains a valid result.

**Receipt and rollback:** Record exact source/config/dependency hashes, executed commands, source/provider/device identities, independent result, exception/skip justification, and rollback/recovery proof. Never mark a packet complete by changing this file’s status.

### VAN-PKT-033 — BE-013: Strategy registry, signed trading modes and qualified edge-consumer contracts

**Exit contract:** list/inspect/promote qualified strategies; produce edge admission evidence; apply Normal/Risky/Aggressive requests; reconcile restart; attribution. Complete real service integration, failure/recovery, migration, source-bound positive/negative and provider tests. Aggressive without admitted edge refuses; mode never raises signed ceilings; both evidence producer and refusal path must be implemented Economic evidence and mode producers are admitted through BE-035 and the forecast integration; no standalone code default substitutes for a signed policy. Rev1.14: every applicable self-optimization/crypto/lean contract and its real evidence must pass; automatic operation and policy evolution are separately qualified.

- VAN-SO-042: Required forecasting plugins are complete integrations, while active selection uses qualified minimal stack.
- VAN-EL-004: Version ex-ante setup/regime definitions and transfer rules; discovery data cannot confirm its own pattern, and sparse or drifting support selects a qualified fallback.

- Exact model epoch, output provenance and replay: Bind requested and resolved model, prompt/data/tool/config versions and response receipts to one qualification epoch; replay the recorded response and reproduce downstream decisions without invoking a provider.
- Target-correct calibration and complete economic qualification: Measure a declared target across the eligible opportunity population, against its base-rate comparator; qualify a controlled positive net contribution using registered dependence, selection, uncertainty and cost procedures.
- Prioritized net-profitability experiments and value-of-information routing: Execute the registered execution-drag, selection, reasoning, event, capital-efficiency and research-economics experiments with controlled positive/null oracles and cost/risk-matched account paths.

- `T-BE-013-NEG` — Aggressive without admitted edge refuses; mode never raises signed ceilings; both evidence producer and refusal path must be implemented
- `T-BE-013-REC` — Crash/restart and lost-response recovery retains durable intent and does not duplicate effects.
- `T-BE-013-SEC` — Wrong owner/account/device/grant and expired/revoked context refuse; logs and projections redact secrets.

- `T-VAN-CX-014-NEG` — A model edits protected execution/risk paths, repeated runs duplicate studies, a failed candidate becomes a promotion opportunity, or an unsigned candidate trades live: refuse.

- `T-VAN-CX-018-NEG` — A child F15/BG independently opens UI, Qlib or Freqtrade sends a live order, or a unavailable provider is labeled healthy: reject.

- `T-VAN-JM-008-POS` — Expose policy/procedural, economic, data, calibration and provider health separately; registered drift/hysteresis rules change a later qualified choice with evidence and exact consumer readback.

**Exit contract:** OD-37 automatic offline review/candidate/evaluation under bounded research grants; complete experiment lineage; exact owner promotion and admitted demotion; qualified evaluator dependency; no qualified candidate is a truthful result. Rev1.14: every applicable self-optimization/crypto/lean contract and its real evidence must pass; automatic operation and policy evolution are separately qualified.

**All deliverables:**
- Candidate proposals from both winners and losers, complete experiments; no autonomous live promotion
- VAN-SO-001: Actual outcome producer, durable evidence, selector and evolution consumers form one integrated VATI loop.
- VAN-SO-002: Operational choices are automatic under qualified grants; novel policies require independent qualification and authorized promotion.
- VAN-SO-003: Evidence update, operational selection and policy mutation are three different typed effects.
- VAN-SO-008: Do not call a mixture of medians an expectation or missing cost zero; record units, estimator and uncertainty.
- VAN-SO-009: Version canonical bucket key, product, side, amount/participation, fees, volatility, event and regime.
- VAN-SO-010: Order finality, cash/fee finality and markout maturity are separate; child messages are not independent trades.
- VAN-SO-011: Live/demo/paper/shadow/backtest/counterfactual provenance stays separate and source-authorized.
- VAN-SO-021: Register actual outcome consumer, persist update and prove later decision effect.
- VAN-SO-022: Observation contribution, consumed revision and cursor/snapshot publish atomically.
- VAN-SO-023: No retroactive editing of issued decision; affected future statistics/models are invalidated or restated.
- VAN-SO-024: Use only fresh mature compatible evidence; pooling/fallback is versioned and justified.
- VAN-SO-025: Qualified hysteresis, tie tolerance, persistence and joint action envelope constrain parameter changes.
- VAN-SO-027: Weekly/drift jobs register target/comparator/budget and every attempted or cancelled candidate.
- VAN-SO-028: Price/time/cost/account oracle and causal tests are prerequisites to candidate efficacy.
- VAN-SO-029: Fit/selection/calibration/test records reflect actual chronology, with overlap controls.
- VAN-SO-031: Each arm has its own cash/reservations/holdings while receiving same permissible information and resource limits.
- VAN-SO-032: No live randomized exploration by default; off-policy claims require propensity/overlap assumptions.
- VAN-SO-033: Qualification binds exact cohort/evaluator/artifact, uncertainty and operational limits; author cannot self-certify.
- VAN-SO-034: New bindings require qualified and authenticated promotion; open parents retain policy unless explicit compatible migration.
- VAN-SO-035: Safety response immediate; economic monitoring uses qualified windows/sequential method; rollback never restores account history.
- VAN-SO-036: Frozen benchmark/horizon includes partial/non-fill costs and explicit fees without double counting.
- VAN-SO-037: Full positive loop is mandatory; real candidate may correctly fail economic tests.
- VAN-SO-039: Learned state can evolve under qualified rule; rule/action/reward/scope changes use policy evolution; risk changes keep own authority.
- VAN-SO-042: Required forecasting plugins are complete integrations, while active selection uses qualified minimal stack.
- VAN-SO-043: DesignChangeProposal binds source/data/evaluator/alternatives/uncertainty, complexity effects and affected contracts.
- VAN-LEAN-002: Each candidate lists operational cost, maintenance/state burden, alternatives and incremental evidence; safety complexity is mandatory.
- VAN-LEAN-005: Astra proposals bind experimental evidence and causal claims; result classes and amendment path explicit.
- VAN-RV-003: Real registered consumer applies newer observation revision once, persists cursor/contribution/snapshot atomically, and later eligible selector consumes its fingerprint. Deliver older revision after correction and conflicting equal revision; older evidence cannot overwrite current contribution and conflict is quarantined. Kill actual process before/after commit, replay bounded journal and verify identical logical snapshot/selection with AS_DECIDED unchanged.
- VAN-RV-006: Reconcile Opus lockout/execution/ledger fixes with canon Jev/temperament/evidence, PR-only Muse/owner controls and their callers; full parity holds. Selecting newer review-input source or main alone must fail retained-feature/caller tests; deleting actual outcome subscription fails later-selection acceptance. Shared-store migrations restore learned state, lockout clocks and account owners; asynchronous Jev outage cannot delay protection.
- VAN-RV-007: Qualified event oracle, immutable licensed inputs, full trial census and availability-time features support matched objective and whole-account arms with predeclared costs and uncertainty. Calendar years alone are not walk-forward; bid data cannot silently become mid; poisoned-future changes to earlier decisions fail; fixed JPY conversion is not current USD P&L. Failed/pruned runs and corrections remain in census; evaluator changes invalidate dependent claims and rerun only affected studies.
- VAN-EL-004: Version ex-ante setup/regime definitions and transfer rules; discovery data cannot confirm its own pattern, and sparse or drifting support selects a qualified fallback.
- VAN-EL-005: Actual outcome producers and registered consumers must change a later supported strategy decision and a qualified execution choice; record distinct evidence and objective scopes and replay both loops.
- VAN-EL-006: Approved learned-state updates operate within current bounds; new policies require independent qualification and current promotion authority, with drift monitoring, expiry, fallback and open-parent recovery.
- Shared Jev consumer scope and asynchronous isolation: Run admitted typed Jev judgments and model research through the actual durable asynchronous path; qualified snapshots improve a later eligible decision while urgent execution/protection continues.
- Exact model epoch, output provenance and replay: Bind requested and resolved model, prompt/data/tool/config versions and response receipts to one qualification epoch; replay the recorded response and reproduce downstream decisions without invoking a provider.
- Target-correct calibration and complete economic qualification: Measure a declared target across the eligible opportunity population, against its base-rate comparator; qualify a controlled positive net contribution using registered dependence, selection, uncertainty and cost procedures.
- Durable global model and research budgets: Atomically reserve bounded per-attempt monetary/token/tool allowance across workers, settle actual costs and reconcile provider quotas with the global ledger.
- Independent review and measured Jev/model contribution: Compare numerical baseline, baseline plus Jev, and separate Argon/DeepSeek challenger arms on preregistered tasks with equal information and independent outcomes; preserve full trial and reviewer provenance.
- Prioritized net-profitability experiments and value-of-information routing: Execute the registered execution-drag, selection, reasoning, event, capital-efficiency and research-economics experiments with controlled positive/null oracles and cost/risk-matched account paths.
- Separate health dimensions, bounded adaptation and owner readback: Expose policy/procedural, economic, data, calibration and provider health separately; registered drift/hysteresis rules change a later qualified choice with evidence and exact consumer readback.

- `T-VAN-SO-032-REC` — Loss of experiment authorization returns to exploitation without affecting safety.
- `T-VAN-SO-033-POS` — Sealed candidate meets registered evidence in appropriate forward/demo environment.
- `T-VAN-SO-033-NEG` — Mocked response, pooled PBO mislabeled per instrument or reward-only success cannot promote.
- `T-VAN-SO-033-REC` — Expired or revoked qualification blocks later activation and exposes reason.
- `T-VAN-SO-034-POS` — New objective uses new epoch; existing parent remains on approved old epoch.
- `T-VAN-SO-034-NEG` — Mid-parent mutable alias switch or mixed model/selector snapshot: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-034-REC` — Crash in epoch swap resolves one binding using durable promotion identity and readback.
- `T-VAN-SO-035-POS` — Degraded policy stops new use and selects still-qualified compatible baseline.
- `T-VAN-SO-035-NEG` — Rollback erases fills, resets lockouts or resurrects revoked evidence: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-035-REC` — No valid fallback keeps new entries closed while positions/protection remain supervised.
- `T-VAN-SO-036-POS` — Known fill and residual opportunity examples reconcile declared loss and actual cash separately.
- `T-VAN-SO-036-NEG` — Never-fill, cancel-late, favorable-horizon choice or rebate-only metric wins by missingness: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-036-REC` — Missing benchmark yields uncertainty/inconclusive, never zero cost.
- `T-VAN-SO-037-POS` — Controlled known-better method changes later selection and actual integration path.
- `T-VAN-SO-037-NEG` — Always-refuse loop or synthetic gain reported as live improvement: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-037-REC` — Negative real evidence preserves baseline and research history.
- `T-VAN-SO-039-POS` — Updated observation changes estimator state but not mandate.
- `T-VAN-SO-039-NEG` — Good returns enlarge parent risk or clear strategy suspension: denied.
- `T-VAN-SO-039-REC` — Restored parameter vector is checked against correct policy/grant version.
- `T-VAN-SO-042-POS` — Each mandated model roundtrip works and may correctly be economically rejected.
- `T-VAN-SO-042-NEG` — Remove a mandated plugin for simplicity or activate unqualified ensemble: fails.
- `T-VAN-SO-042-REC` — Model expiry/outage leaves independent qualified execution fallback.
- `T-VAN-SO-043-POS` — Controlled evidence produces reviewable precise contract amendment.
- `T-VAN-SO-043-NEG` — Misleading no-edge assertion, run cherry-picking or no evaluator receipt cannot authorize design replacement.
- `T-VAN-SO-043-REC` — Corrected dataset triggers proposal re-evaluation with original result retained.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-002-POS` — Simpler active baseline retained when challenger offers no material benefit.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-002-NEG` — More components or higher backtest Sharpe without cost/ablation evidence cannot justify activation.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-002-REC` — Removal/fallback restores required capability and retains history.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-005-POS` — Valid controlled study produces precise revisable design recommendation.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-005-NEG` — Backtest PASS self-signs policy or changes live mandate: denied.
- `T-VAN-LEAN-005-REC` — Corrected result withdraws recommendation and dependent candidate qualification.
- `T-VAN-RV-003-POS` — Real registered consumer applies newer observation revision once, persists cursor/contribution/snapshot atomically, and later eligible selector consumes its fingerprint.
- `T-VAN-RV-003-NEG` — Deliver older revision after correction and conflicting equal revision; older evidence cannot overwrite current contribution and conflict is quarantined.
- `T-VAN-RV-003-REC` — Kill actual process before/after commit, replay bounded journal and verify identical logical snapshot/selection with AS_DECIDED unchanged.
- `T-VAN-RV-006-POS` — Reconcile Opus lockout/execution/ledger fixes with canon Jev/temperament/evidence, PR-only Muse/owner controls and their callers; full parity holds.
- `T-VAN-RV-006-NEG` — Selecting newer review-input source or main alone must fail retained-feature/caller tests; deleting actual outcome subscription fails later-selection acceptance.
- `T-VAN-RV-006-REC` — Shared-store migrations restore learned state, lockout clocks and account owners; asynchronous Jev outage cannot delay protection.
- `T-VAN-RV-007-POS` — Qualified event oracle, immutable licensed inputs, full trial census and availability-time features support matched objective and whole-account arms with predeclared costs and uncertainty.
- `T-VAN-RV-007-NEG` — Calendar years alone are not walk-forward; bid data cannot silently become mid; poisoned-future changes to earlier decisions fail; fixed JPY conversion is not current USD P&L.
- `T-VAN-RV-007-REC` — Failed/pruned runs and corrections remain in census; evaluator changes invalidate dependent claims and rerun only affected studies.
- `EL-AT-007-POS` — A regime shift, sparse cohort or incompatible market exposes unsupported transfer. The selector reports insufficient/stale support and uses a qualified fallback; later fresh qualifying evidence permits recovery.
- `EL-AT-007-NEG` — Unsupported transfer and hindsight classification are rejected; qualified fallback or abstention is explicit.
- `EL-AT-007-REC` — Retain the safe fallback through drift, admit a newly qualified cohort snapshot and record the exact boundary at which supported adaptation resumes.
- `EL-AT-003-POS` — A controlled supported signal is paired with a bad execution method. Independent cash/cost oracles identify the execution contribution; a qualified replacement improves execution under equal constraints without claiming new alpha.
- `EL-AT-003-NEG` — Independent decomposition and matched-objective/account comparison detect the confounding or accounting error.
- `EL-AT-003-REC` — Correct the execution measurement, restate affected observations and re-evaluate the bounded method on the same valid objectives before qualified activation.
- `EL-AT-004-POS` — A repeatable controlled regime-specific strategy opportunity is discovered only in development data, confirmed on protected chronological data, qualified and bound in an admitted test environment; a later matching decision changes through the real strategy consumer.
- `EL-AT-004-NEG` — The protected evaluation and actual-consumer integration checks fail; no binding is created.
- `EL-AT-004-REC` — Version the repaired strategy and data boundary, use a fresh protected confirmation window, then bind in the admitted test environment and reproduce a later choice.
- `EL-AT-012-POS` — Removing the actual strategy or execution outcome consumer makes the corresponding later-choice integration test fail. Restoring it recovers from the durable cursor; a direct test-only observe call cannot satisfy acceptance.
- `EL-AT-012-NEG` — Each named later-choice integration test fails for the missing consumer; refusal-only behaviour cannot pass.
- `EL-AT-012-REC` — Restore each registered subscription, replay its bounded durable backlog and verify the next eligible choices use the recovered snapshots exactly once.
- `EL-AT-011-POS` — Unqualified, expired, revoked and wrong-account changes fail at the binding/consumer boundary; an authorized compatible fallback and open-parent reconciliation remain available.
- `EL-AT-011-NEG` — The native binding boundary rejects before effect; open positions retain protection and reconciliation.
- `EL-AT-011-REC` — Reconcile pending parents, use an independently valid compatible fallback, and resume only with a fresh authorized binding; revoked artifacts remain revoked.
- `T-VAN-JM-001-POS` — Run admitted typed Jev judgments and model research through the actual durable asynchronous path; qualified snapshots improve a later eligible decision while urgent execution/protection continues.
- `T-VAN-JM-001-NEG` — Stall the model, saturate its queue, inject an attention-module result into a trading consumer and set apply_effect=true on wrong-scope evidence; no risk/stop/reconcile wait or unauthorized effect is permitted.
- `T-VAN-JM-001-REC` — Restart the isolated worker, replay the original work IDs within bounded backlog and expire obsolete responses; preserve open-parent protection and the baseline.
- `T-VAN-JM-002-POS` — Bind requested and resolved model, prompt/data/tool/config versions and response receipts to one qualification epoch; replay the recorded response and reproduce downstream decisions without invoking a provider.
- `T-VAN-JM-002-NEG` — Silently redirect an alias, change prompt/retrieval/calibration or supply a wrong-context response; prior qualification cannot authorize the changed identity.
- `T-VAN-JM-002-REC` — Quarantine unknown identity, retain old decisions, requalify the new epoch and atomically activate a compatible binding with authoritative readback.
- `T-VAN-JM-004-POS` — Measure a declared target across the eligible opportunity population, against its base-rate comparator; qualify a controlled positive net contribution using registered dependence, selection, uncertainty and cost procedures.
- `T-VAN-JM-004-NEG` — Submit only 50 correlated disagreements, missing calibration, positive gross means with negative net costs, or a change-helped score labeled trade-win probability; probability-dependent/economic admission fails.
- `T-VAN-JM-004-REC` — Restore full trials and missingness accounting, recompute target-specific calibration and confidence on new protected data; retain explanation-only use only where independently qualified.
- `T-VAN-JM-005-POS` — Atomically reserve bounded per-attempt monetary/token/tool allowance across workers, settle actual costs and reconcile provider quotas with the global ledger.
- `T-VAN-JM-005-NEG` — Race workers, exhaust budget with malformed/failed outputs, trigger fallback retries or cancel after provider acceptance; no unreserved call or reset of costs is allowed and unknown bills retain liability.
- `T-VAN-JM-005-REC` — Crash before/after reservation and settlement, recover the same attempt IDs and reconcile unknown charges without double billing or duplicate provider work; use qualified no-model fallback.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-POS` — Compare numerical baseline, baseline plus Jev, and separate Argon/DeepSeek challenger arms on preregistered tasks with equal information and independent outcomes; preserve full trial and reviewer provenance.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-NEG` — Leak author/model identity through nested fields or free text, reuse the holdout, let the proposer accept its own result or count correlated model agreement as independent proof; fail review/qualification.
- `T-VAN-JM-006-REC` — Replace tainted review packets, retain rejected trials and failed canaries, re-establish separate review/holdout access and repeat evaluation with a fresh protected window.
- `T-VAN-JM-007-POS` — Execute the registered execution-drag, selection, reasoning, event, capital-efficiency and research-economics experiments with controlled positive/null oracles and cost/risk-matched account paths.
- `T-VAN-JM-007-NEG` — Promote a leaderboard score, increased leverage, fill-only gains or an expensive model with negative incremental net utility; reject unsupported benefit and prevent expanding tools or risk to manufacture a win.
- `T-VAN-JM-007-REC` — Retain the simpler qualified baseline, revise only within the registered research budget and evaluate a frozen new hypothesis on fresh evidence; inconclusive remains a valid result.
- `T-VAN-JM-008-POS` — Expose policy/procedural, economic, data, calibration and provider health separately; registered drift/hysteresis rules change a later qualified choice with evidence and exact consumer readback.
- `T-VAN-JM-008-NEG` — Mark a consistently losing policy healthy solely because it obeys rules, rewrite policy after one ordinary loss, hide stale model identity/costs or reactivate a revoked fallback; appropriate gates fail.
- `T-VAN-JM-008-REC` — Keep urgent protection during suspension, reconcile open parents and corrected labels, and resume only a currently qualified compatible binding with durable owner-visible status.

**Receipt and rollback:** Record exact source/config/dependency hashes, executed commands, source/provider/device identities, independent result, exception/skip justification, and rollback/recovery proof. Never mark a packet complete by changing this file’s status.

### VAN-PKT-085 — BE-033: Backend deployment, recovery, capacity and observability

- `T-HVA-013-NEG` — Use string no as true, skip remaining checks for one survivor, or replace missing data with zero; reject.
- `T-HVA-013-REC` — A rule-version update rechecks affected candidates from retained facts and does not overwrite prior decisions.
- `T-HVA-014-POS` — Recheck a changed security/route state immediately while leaving unchanged rejected candidates within their valid cooldown.
- `T-HVA-014-NEG` — Keep a previous rejection forever after a changed authority, or requeue without bounds on each duplicate event; detect.
